IF EXISTS (select * from sysobjects where name = 'trg_access_I')
DROP TRIGGER trg_access_I
GO

CREATE TRIGGER trg_access_I
ON access /* name of the target table  */
FOR INSERT
AS
BEGIN
  INSERT access_log(log_ipaddr, log_date, log_appname, log_hostname, log_activity) 
  SELECT client_net_address, SYSDATETIME(), app_name(), host_name() , 'insert'
  FROM sys.dm_exec_connections dec
  WHERE dec.session_id = @@SPID;
END

GO

IF EXISTS (select * from sysobjects where name = 'trg_access_U')
DROP TRIGGER trg_access_U
GO

CREATE TRIGGER trg_access_U
ON access /* name of the target table  */
FOR UPDATE
AS
BEGIN
  INSERT access_log(log_ipaddr, log_date, log_appname, log_hostname, log_activity) 
  SELECT client_net_address, SYSDATETIME(), app_name(), host_name() , 'update'
  FROM sys.dm_exec_connections dec
  WHERE dec.session_id = @@SPID;
END

GO

IF EXISTS (select * from sysobjects where name = 'trg_access_D')
DROP TRIGGER trg_access_D

  SELECT client_net_address, SYSDATETIME(), app_name(), host_name() , 'insert'
  FROM sys.dm_exec_connections dec
  WHERE dec.session_id = @@SPID;
END

GO

END

GO

IF EXISTS (select * from sysobjects where name = 'trg_overtime_payroll_details_U')
DROP TRIGGER trg_overtime_payroll_details_U
GO

CREATE TRIGGER trg_overtime_payroll_details_U
ON overtime_payroll_details /* name of the target table  */
FOR UPDATE
AS
BEGIN
  INSERT overtime_payroll_details_log(log_ipaddr, log_date, log_appname, log_hostname, log_activity) 
  SELECT client_net_address, SYSDATETIME(), app_name(), host_name() , 'update'
  FROM sys.dm_exec_connections dec
  WHERE dec.session_id = @@SPID;
END

GO
